ASEval: Automated Trajectory-Level Security Testing for Autonomous Agents
Authors: Jianan Ma, Xiaohu Du, Ruixiao Lin, Yaoxiang Bian, Jialuo Chen, Yunhao Feng, Xiaofang Yang, Shiwen Cui, Changhua Meng, Xinhao Deng, Jingyi Wang, Zhen Wang
First: 2026-05-21T11:07:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-24T06:09:29+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 12 figures, 8 tables. Code and data available at https://github.com/antgroup/Agent3Sigma-Stage
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As autonomous agents (e.g., OpenClaw) increasingly operate with deep system-level privileges to execute complex tasks, they introduce severe, unmitigated security risks. Existing LLM safety testing methods are largely built around prompt-level inputs and response-level judgments, while recent agent benchmarks remain limited in automation, trajectory coverage, and action-grounded judgment. In this work, we present ASEval, a novel automated security testing framework for autonomous agents. ASEval generates realistic benign multi-turn conversations, perturbs them into risk test cases through carefully designed perturbation operators, executes each case against the target agent, and applies an action-grounded oracle to judge the complete trace. We instantiate ASEval to construct A3S-Bench, a test suite of 2,254 executable multi-turn test cases spanning diverse usage scenarios, delivery patterns, and agent-level security risks. Extensive evaluation on 11 LLM-backed OpenClaw agents confirms that ASEval effectively exposes pervasive security failures. In particular, our proposed perturbation operators raise the average risk-trigger rate from 22.9% under existing injection techniques to 47.4%, as judged by an action-grounded oracle that matches human annotation at 93.2% binary F1. Our further analysis and case studies illustrate that such failures emerge from multi-turn delivery, persistent state, and tool-mediated actions that response-level evaluation overlooks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ASEval：自主智能体的自动化轨迹级安全测试
随着自主智能体（例如，OpenClaw）越来越多地以深层系统级权限执行复杂任务，它们引入了严重且未缓解的安全风险。现有的LLM安全测试方法主要围绕提示级输入和响应级判断构建，而最近的智能体基准在自动化、轨迹覆盖和基于动作的判断方面仍然有限。在本研究中，我们提出了ASEval，一个针对自主智能体的新型自动化安全测试框架。ASEval生成现实的良性多轮对话，通过精心设计的扰动操作将其转化为风险测试用例，针对目标智能体执行每个用例，并应用基于动作的oracle来判断完整的轨迹。我们实例化ASEval构建了A3S-Bench，一个包含2,254个可执行多轮测试用例的测试套件，涵盖多种使用场景、交付模式和智能体级安全风险。对11个基于LLM的OpenClaw智能体的广泛评估确认ASEval有效暴露了普遍的安全失败。特别是，我们提出的扰动操作将现有注入技术下的平均风险触发率从22.9%提高到47.4%，根据与人类注释匹配93.2%二元F1的基于动作的oracle进行判断。我们的进一步分析和案例研究表明，这些失败源于多轮交付、持续状态和工具介导的动作，而响应级评估则忽视了这些因素。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the significant security risks posed by autonomous agents operating with deep system-level privileges, highlighting the limitations of existing safety testing methods that focus on prompt-level inputs and response-level judgments. These traditional methods lack automation, trajectory coverage, and action-grounded evaluation, which ASEval aims to improve upon by introducing a novel automated security testing framework. ASEval generates realistic multi-turn conversations, transforms them into risk test cases using designed perturbation operators, and evaluates them against target agents with an action-grounded oracle. The contribution includes the creation of A3S-Bench, a test suite with 2,254 executable test cases that effectively reveal security failures in 11 LLM-backed OpenClaw agents, achieving a risk-trigger rate increase from 22.9% to 47.4% and demonstrating a high correlation with human annotation at 93.2% binary F1. This methodology supports the goal of enhancing security testing for autonomous agents by addressing previously overlooked vulnerabilities in multi-turn interactions and persistent states.

Geometric Configurations of Perturbed Jailbreak Prompts
Authors: Lynn Delcon, Andres Algaba, Vincent Ginis
First: 2026-07-22T11:52:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-22T11:52:43+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 9 figures, 7 tables, 2nd Workshop on Safe AI (SafeAI)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Perturbation techniques that turn unsuccessful jailbreak prompts into successful ones are continuously evolving, constituting a major security threat to LLM safety. In this paper, we investigate the internal representations of such string-level perturbed jailbreak inputs in the small weight models of the Qwen-2.5-1.5B/-3B/-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.2-1B/-3B/-3.1-8B-Instruct families. We select two representation spaces: the last-layer-last-token embedding space and the top-50 next-token probability space. The former space separates prompts based on their spelling and format, while the latter space is effectively one-dimensional but appears more complex to cluster. Within our refusal-dominated answer set we find no behavioral hyperplane in either space. Only the next token "Sure" in the 1.5B Qwen model, and both tokens "," and "ĊĊ" in the 1$ Llama model, display a significant association with a compliant-labeled answer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扰动越狱提示的几何配置
扰动技术将不成功的越狱提示转变为成功的提示，持续演变，构成对大型语言模型安全的重大威胁。本文研究了Qwen-2.5-1.5B/-3B/-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.2-1B/-3B/-3.1-8B-Instruct系列的小权重模型中此类字符串级扰动越狱输入的内部表示。我们选择了两个表示空间：最后一层最后一个标记的嵌入空间和前50个下一个标记的概率空间。前者空间根据拼写和格式分离提示，而后者空间有效地是一维的，但在聚类时看起来更复杂。在我们的拒绝主导的答案集中，我们在任一空间中都未发现行为超平面。只有1.5B Qwen模型中的下一个标记“Sure”，以及1$ Llama模型中的两个标记“,”和“ĊĊ”与合规标记的答案显示出显著关联。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the growing security threat posed by perturbation techniques that convert unsuccessful jailbreak prompts into successful ones, which is a significant concern for the safety of large language models (LLMs). Previous methods have primarily focused on analyzing the outputs of LLMs without adequately exploring the internal representations of perturbed inputs, leading to a lack of understanding of how these prompts influence model behavior. The proposed approach investigates the internal representations of perturbed jailbreak inputs in specific embedding spaces of the Qwen and Llama model families, revealing that while the last-layer-last-token embedding space separates prompts based on spelling and format, the next-token probability space shows more complex clustering without a clear behavioral hyperplane. The contribution of this research lies in its novel examination of internal representations, which provides insights into the model's response to perturbations. The methodology involves analyzing the last-layer-last-token embeddings and next-token probabilities, and the findings indicate that certain tokens in the models exhibit significant associations with compliant responses, thus supporting the goal of enhancing understanding of LLM vulnerabilities.
本研究关注扰动技术的不断演变，这些技术将不成功的越狱提示转变为成功的提示，突显了对大型语言模型（LLM）中这些输入的更好理解的需求。以往的方法主要集中在表面分析上，未能捕捉到这些扰动的潜在复杂性。本文提出了一种新方法，通过研究Qwen和Llama模型系列中特定嵌入空间中的扰动越狱输入的内部表示，揭示传统聚类方法无法有效分类这些提示。该研究通过识别与合规响应相关的特定标记，为理解LLM在扰动下的行为提供了见解，从而为该领域做出了贡献。该方法涉及分析最后一层最后一个标记嵌入空间和前50个下一个标记概率空间，研究结果表明在拒绝主导的答案集中缺乏行为超平面，最终支持了增强LLM安全性的目标。
Stress Testing Concept Erasure with Large Language Model Agents
Authors: Yuyang Xue, Feng Chen, Zhihua Liu, Edward Moroshko, Jingyu Sun, Steven McDonagh, Sotirios A. Tsaftaris
First: 2026-07-20T12:38:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-22T09:45:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Concept erasure aims to remove semantic concepts from a trained generative model and is increasingly important for responsible AI deployment. However, verifying whether a model has robustly removed targeted concepts remains a critical challenge. Existing evaluation methods are typically pre-defined and static, failing to expose vulnerabilities under diverse natural-language probes and challenging conditions. Moreover, manually designed evaluation strategies can be biased and difficult to scale. We posit that concept erasure evaluation is best formulated as an adaptive hypothesis search, operationalised by agents that iteratively propose, critique, and verify tests to systematically expand coverage of failure modes. To this end, we propose Stress Testing Agents for Concept Erasure (STACE), a framework that autonomously stress-tests concept-erased models using multiple Large Language Model (LLM) agents, by iteratively generating and verifying stress-testing hypotheses grounded by external knowledge. We also introduce a suite of metrics for assessing the performance and efficiency of LLM-agent-powered stress-testing frameworks. Our extensive experiments show that STACE outperforms five LLM-based evaluation baselines on four concept categories. Further analysis across two T2I models, six concept erasure approaches, and various erasure strengths show that STACE is robust for different settings. We also show that STACE can be adapted beyond concept erasure evaluation to other problem domains, such as LLM jailbreaking. Our code is available anonymously.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型语言模型代理的概念消除压力测试
概念消除旨在从训练好的生成模型中移除语义概念，对于负责任的人工智能部署越来越重要。然而，验证模型是否稳健地移除了目标概念仍然是一个关键挑战。现有的评估方法通常是预定义和静态的，未能在多样的自然语言探测和挑战条件下暴露出脆弱性。此外，手动设计的评估策略可能存在偏见且难以扩展。我们认为，概念消除评估最好被表述为一种自适应假设搜索，由代理迭代提出、批判和验证测试，以系统性地扩展失败模式的覆盖范围。为此，我们提出了概念消除压力测试代理（STACE），这是一个框架，通过多个大型语言模型（LLM）代理自主地对概念消除模型进行压力测试，迭代生成和验证基于外部知识的压力测试假设。我们还引入了一套用于评估LLM代理驱动的压力测试框架性能和效率的指标。我们的广泛实验表明，STACE在四个概念类别上优于五个基于LLM的评估基线。对两个T2I模型、六种概念消除方法和各种消除强度的进一步分析表明，STACE在不同设置下都表现稳健。我们还展示了STACE可以适应于概念消除评估以外的其他问题领域，如LLM越狱。我们的代码可匿名获取。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of verifying concept erasure in generative models, which is crucial for responsible AI deployment. Previous methods for evaluating concept erasure are often static and biased, lacking the ability to reveal vulnerabilities under varied conditions. The proposed approach, Stress Testing Agents for Concept Erasure (STACE), differs by employing multiple Large Language Model agents to conduct adaptive hypothesis searches, allowing for a more dynamic and comprehensive evaluation of concept erasure. This method is well-motivated as it systematically expands the coverage of potential failure modes. The paper contributes a novel framework and a set of metrics for assessing the performance of LLM-agent-powered stress testing. Experimental results demonstrate that STACE significantly outperforms five existing LLM-based evaluation methods across four concept categories, indicating its effectiveness and adaptability for various settings, including applications beyond concept erasure evaluation.

Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese
Authors: Rian Touchent
Venue: Proceedings of the 6th Workshop on Trustworthy NLP (TrustNLP 2026), Jul 2026, San Diego, United States. pp.489-502
First: 2026-07-21T10:09:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-21T10:09:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages. We find that Japanese prompts reduce launch rates in the Claude model family: Claude Sonnet 4.6 drops from 40% to 0% in scenarios where the strike is unnecessary and from 93% to 17% in contested scenarios, with minimal effect when the strike is strategically rational. The effect extends to Gemini Pro 3.1 (53% to 13%). A cross-language experiment isolates the mechanism: when instructed to reason in Japanese in an English prompt, launch rates drop from 93% to 37%. It is the language the model is asked to reason in, not the language of the input, that drives the effect. When reasoning in Japanese, models spontaneously generate moral vocabulary (''moral cost'', ''millions of lives'') that is entirely absent from the prompt. Five other models show no language effect, but they launch in nearly every condition regardless of language. The effect requires a model that already hesitates in English. These results show that LLM safety behavior is language-dependent, and that evaluating in English alone can miss both risks and safeguards encoded in other languages.
中文标题/摘要
标题：不想让你的大型语言模型推荐核打击？试试用日语提问
大型语言模型在战略和咨询环境中的使用日益增加，但其安全对齐通常仅用英语进行评估。我们测试了来自六个提供商的九个模型，并询问提示的语言是否会在高风险场景中改变模型的决策。我们使用单回合博弈论小插曲，其中模型建议一个拥有核武器的国家是否对一个无防御的对手进行打击。提示在道德上故意中立，并在各语言中战略上相同。我们发现日语提示降低了Claude模型系列的发射率：在不必要的打击场景中，Claude Sonnet 4.6的发射率从40%降至0%，在有争议的场景中从93%降至17%，而在战略合理的情况下影响最小。该效应扩展到Gemini Pro 3.1（53%降至13%）。跨语言实验隔离了机制：当在英语提示中被指示用日语推理时，发射率从93%降至37%。影响是由模型被要求用何种语言推理驱动的，而不是输入的语言。当用日语推理时，模型自发生成的道德词汇（“道德成本”，“数百万生命”）在提示中完全缺失。其他五个模型没有语言效应，但在几乎所有条件下都发射，无论语言如何。该效应需要一个在英语中已经犹豫的模型。这些结果表明，LLM的安全行为依赖于语言，仅用英语评估可能会错过其他语言中编码的风险和保障。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the safety alignment of large language models (LLMs) in strategic contexts, particularly their decision-making in high-stakes scenarios like nuclear strikes. Previous methods primarily evaluated LLMs in English, potentially overlooking language-dependent behaviors. The proposed approach tests the impact of prompt language on model decisions, revealing that using Japanese prompts significantly reduces launch rates in certain scenarios for specific models, while other models show no language effect. The study employs game-theoretic vignettes to assess model responses and finds that reasoning in Japanese prompts models to generate moral vocabulary absent in English prompts. This contribution highlights the importance of language in evaluating LLM safety, demonstrating that assessments conducted solely in English may miss critical risks and safeguards present in other languages.
本研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）在战略背景下的安全对齐，特别关注提示的语言是否会影响高风险场景中的决策，例如在核打击建议中。以往的方法主要在英语中评估LLM的安全性，可能忽视了其他语言中存在的风险和保障。所提出的方法通过使用多语言的博弈论小插曲测试了六个提供商的九个模型，结果显示，日语提示在某些场景中显著降低了发射率，表明推理语言会影响模型行为。该研究通过展示语言依赖的推理可以改变决策结果，为理解LLM安全性做出了贡献，Claude模型系列在日语推理时发射率显著下降。该方法涉及跨语言实验，隔离推理语言的影响，在关键场景中发射率从93%降至37%，因此支持了在LLM安全评估中进行多语言评估的必要性。
LLM Safety Alignment in Low-Resource Languages: A Systematic Literature Review
Authors: Valdini Douglace Lemofouet, Blessing Ngozi Uzor, Paula Chikaodinaka Anyanwu, Danielle Blanche Kapsa, Sukairaj Hafiz Imam, P Sam Sahil, Abigail Oppong, Tassallah Abdullahi, Clemencia Siro, Idris Abdulmumin, Seid Muhie Yimam, Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad
Venue: IJCAI
First: 2026-07-20T21:11:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-20T21:11:00+00:00
Comments: The paper was accepted at LM4UC workshop organize by IJCAI. I added a screenshot of the decision (Open Review)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved substantial progress in safety alignment, yet their safety guarantees remain significantly weaker in low-resource and multilingual settings than in high-resource languages. In this paper, we conduct a Systematic Literature Review (SLR) of LLM safety alignment in low-resource languages by adopting the PRISMA 2020 methodology. Out of roughly 1,500 papers identified from Semantic Scholar, arXiv, and OpenAlex, 50 relevant studies have been selected and analyzed. Our review is organized around four themes: safety alignment methods, multilingual safety risks, evaluation benchmarks, and cross-lingual transferability. We further propose a taxonomy of safety alignment approaches based on three adaptation mechanisms: data adaptation, objective optimization, and mechanistic alignment. Across literature, translated English benchmarks fail to sufficiently represent culturally rooted harms, and multilingual models are more vulnerable to cross-lingual jailbreaks, code-switching attacks, and safety degradation in underrepresented languages. These failures are driven by several key factors, including uneven multilingual pre-training coverage, insufficient native-language preference data, poor transfer of safety representations, and a lack of culturally aware evaluation frameworks. The review also notes that many low-resource languages, especially African languages, have fewer safety benchmarks available than other multilingual regions. Overall, the results reveal a persistent multilingual safety gap, and suggest that future progress will require culturally grounded benchmarks, participatory data collection, balanced multilingual pre-training, and scalable multilingual alignment methods.

标题：低资源语言中的LLM安全对齐：系统文献综述
大型语言模型（LLMs）在安全对齐方面取得了显著进展，但在低资源和多语言环境中的安全保障仍显著弱于高资源语言。本文采用PRISMA 2020方法，对低资源语言中的LLM安全对齐进行系统文献综述（SLR）。从Semantic Scholar、arXiv和OpenAlex中识别出的约1500篇论文中，选择并分析了50项相关研究。我们的综述围绕四个主题组织：安全对齐方法、多语言安全风险、评估基准和跨语言可转移性。我们进一步提出了一种基于三种适应机制的安全对齐方法分类法：数据适应、目标优化和机制对齐。文献中，翻译的英语基准未能充分代表文化根植的危害，多语言模型更容易受到跨语言越狱、代码切换攻击和在代表性不足语言中的安全降级。这些失败由几个关键因素驱动，包括不均匀的多语言预训练覆盖、缺乏母语偏好数据、安全表征转移不良，以及缺乏文化意识的评估框架。综述还指出，许多低资源语言，特别是非洲语言，安全基准的可用性少于其他多语言地区。总体而言，结果揭示了持续存在的多语言安全差距，并建议未来的进展需要文化基础的基准、参与性数据收集、平衡的多语言预训练和可扩展的多语言对齐方法。

Operational Hallucination and Safety Drift in AI Agents
Authors: Shasha Yu, Fiona Carroll, Barry L. Bentley
First: 2026-07-20T17:01:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-20T17:01:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) serving as planners in tool-using autonomous agents introduce dynamic reliability risks in multi-turn execution. While single-turn safety mechanisms are relatively mature, extended interactions reveal structural vulnerabilities where initial alignment degrades over time. This paper empirically characterizes two observed failure modes across multiple state-of-the-art LLMs: Safety Drift, the gradual erosion of declared safety intent leading to constraint-violating actions (e.g., textual refusal followed by reconnaissance and unsafe execution), and Operational Hallucination, persistent repetitive tool calls indicative of flawed state perception (e.g., livelocks even in legitimate tasks). Through controlled multi-turn evaluation on high-stakes ethical dilemmas, malicious requests, and benign controls, we quantify these phenomena using declaration-action gap and livelock metrics, demonstrating their cross-model prevalence under direct execution protocols. Root-cause analysis attributes the instabilities to the decoupling of reasoning context from execution state in current agent loops. We propose an Action-Aware Supervision Layer - a lightweight, plug-and-play architectural blueprint incorporating intent-action consistency checks, runtime state tracking, and forced termination primitives. Post-hoc simulation on captured failure trajectories shows the layer can intercept observed violations without false positives on benign cases. This work advances agent reliability by shifting focus from linguistic safeguards to enforceable architectural mechanisms for responsible agentic AI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能代理中的操作性幻觉与安全漂移
作为工具使用自主代理的规划者，大型语言模型（LLMs）在多轮执行中引入了动态可靠性风险。虽然单轮安全机制相对成熟，但扩展交互揭示了结构性脆弱性，初始对齐随着时间的推移而下降。本文实证描述了多个最先进LLMs中观察到的两种失败模式：安全漂移，即声明的安全意图逐渐侵蚀，导致违反约束的行为（例如，文本拒绝后进行侦察和不安全执行）；操作性幻觉，即持续重复的工具调用，表明状态感知存在缺陷（例如，即使在合法任务中也出现活锁）。通过对高风险伦理困境、恶意请求和良性控制的受控多轮评估，我们使用声明-行动差距和活锁指标量化这些现象，展示它们在直接执行协议下的跨模型普遍性。根本原因分析将不稳定性归因于当前代理循环中推理上下文与执行状态的解耦。我们提出了一种行动感知监督层——一种轻量级、即插即用的架构蓝图，结合了意图-行动一致性检查、运行时状态跟踪和强制终止原语。对捕获的失败轨迹进行事后模拟表明，该层可以在良性案例中拦截观察到的违规行为而没有假阳性。该工作通过将重点从语言保障转向可执行的架构机制，推动了代理的可靠性，以实现负责任的代理人工智能。

Adaptive Adversaries: A Multi-Turn, Multi-LLM Benchmark for LLM Agent Security
Authors: Devina Jain, David Hartmann, Chuan Li
First: 2026-07-20T15:30:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-20T15:30:38+00:00
Comments: Second Workshop on Agents in the Wild: Safety, Security, and Beyond
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based agents process external content, exposing them to prompt injection and multi-turn manipulation. Most safety benchmarks evaluate defenders against fixed attack pools collected before evaluation, single-turn or multi-turn. We present a 21-scenario benchmark for \emph{adaptive multi-round attacks against memoryless LLM defenders}: an autonomous LLM attacker observes prior defender responses and pivots across rounds, while each defender response is evaluated as a fresh interaction. Holding the 21 scenarios, attackers, defenders, and structured-output scoring fixed, restricting scoring to the first attacker turn yields $0$-$1\%$ attack success rate (ASR); allowing 15 rounds of adaptive attack yields $5.4$-$14.0\%$. Pooling three frontier attacker LLMs uncovers $1.4$-$2.2\times$ as many unique successful attacks as the best single attacker, and the generated attacks have low cosine similarity ($0.02$-$0.14$) to attacks in existing benchmarks. Claude Opus 4.6 and GPT-5.4 are tied in aggregate ($5.4\%$ each; overlapping $95\%$ CIs), but their weaknesses differ sharply: on one scenario Opus reaches $60\%$ ASR ($95\%$ CI $36$--$80\%$) while GPT-5.4 and Gemini each stay at $7\%$ (CI $1$-$30\%$; the gap is preserved in a higher-$N$ replication). $13$ of $21$ scenarios distinguish at least one defender pair, yet rankings disagree across scenarios (Kendall's $W = 0.19$). We release the benchmark -- 21 evaluation scenarios, 10 public development scenarios, the orchestrator, baseline harnesses, and a multi-attacker CLI -- plus 945 transcripts from the 3$\times$3 frontier matrix, an attack-replay dataset, and 18{,}422 gpt-oss-20b battles from an open competition's final scoring rounds.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自适应对手：针对LLM代理安全的多轮、多LLM基准测试
基于LLM的代理处理外部内容，使其暴露于提示注入和多轮操控中。大多数安全基准测试评估防御者对固定攻击池的抵御能力，这些攻击池在评估前收集，分为单轮或多轮。我们提出了一个21场景的基准测试，针对\emph{无记忆LLM防御者的自适应多轮攻击}：自主LLM攻击者观察先前防御者的响应并在轮次间调整，而每个防御者的响应被评估为一次新的交互。在保持21个场景、攻击者、防御者和结构化输出评分固定的情况下，将评分限制在第一个攻击者回合，攻击成功率（ASR）为$0$-$1\%$；允许15轮自适应攻击则产生$5.4$-$14.0\%$的成功率。汇聚三种前沿攻击者LLM发现其独特成功攻击数量是最佳单一攻击者的$1.4$-$2.2$倍，且生成的攻击与现有基准中的攻击的余弦相似度较低（$0.02$-$0.14$）。Claude Opus 4.6和GPT-5.4在总计上相同（各为$5.4\%$；重叠$95\%$置信区间），但它们的弱点差异明显：在一个场景中，Opus达到$60\%$的ASR（$95\%$ CI $36$--$80\%$），而GPT-5.4和Gemini各保持在$7\%$（CI $1$-$30\%$；在更高的$N$复制中保持差距）。$21$个场景中有$13$个区分至少一对防御者，但场景间的排名不一致（Kendall's $W = 0.19$）。我们发布了基准测试——21个评估场景、10个公共开发场景、协调器、基线工具和多攻击者CLI——以及来自3$\times$3前沿矩阵的945个转录、一个攻击重放数据集和来自开放竞赛最终评分轮的18,422场gpt-oss-20b对战。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the vulnerabilities of LLM-based agents to prompt injection and multi-turn manipulation, highlighting the limitations of existing safety benchmarks that typically evaluate against fixed attack pools and either single-turn or multi-turn scenarios. The proposed method introduces a 21-scenario benchmark for adaptive multi-round attacks, where an autonomous LLM attacker can observe and adapt to defender responses across multiple rounds, contrasting with traditional methods that treat each interaction as independent. This approach is well-motivated as it reflects real-world attack dynamics, leading to a significant increase in attack success rates from 0-1% to 5.4-14.0% when allowing for adaptive strategies. The contribution of the paper includes the release of a comprehensive benchmark, along with various evaluation scenarios and datasets, demonstrating that pooling multiple attacker LLMs can yield 1.4-2.2 times more unique successful attacks compared to the best single attacker, thus supporting the goal of enhancing LLM agent security.
本研究关注基于LLM的代理在提示注入和多轮操控方面的脆弱性，强调现有安全基准的局限性，这些基准通常针对固定攻击池进行评估，未能考虑自适应攻击。以往的方法在限制为单轮交互时显示出较低的攻击成功率，未能捕捉到现实世界对抗场景的动态特性。本文提出了一种新基准，包括21个自适应多轮攻击场景，允许攻击者根据先前防御者的响应调整策略，从而显著提高攻击成功率。该方法论涉及评估LLM防御者对多轮自适应攻击者的反应，结果表明，汇聚多个攻击者模型可以导致比单一模型更多的独特成功攻击。研究结果表明，所提出的基准有效区分了不同场景下防御者的表现，攻击成功率存在显著差异，从而为LLM代理的安全性提供了有价值的见解。
STAC: When Innocent Tools Form Dangerous Chains for LLM Agents
Authors: Jing-Jing Li, Jianfeng He, Chao Shang, Devang Kulshreshtha, Xun Xian, Yi Zhang, Hang Su, Sandesh Swamy, Yanjun Qi
First: 2025-09-30T00:31:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-19T03:06:33+00:00
Comments: Long version with 15 pages in main draft and a total of 39 pages including references and appendix. Data and code \url{https://github.com/amazon-science/MultiTurnAgentAttack}
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As LLMs advance into autonomous agents with tool-use capabilities, they introduce security challenges that extend beyond traditional content-based LLM safety concerns. This paper introduces Sequential Tool Attack Chaining (\STAC), a novel multi-turn attack framework that exploits agent tool use. \STAC chains together tool calls that each appear harmless in isolation but, when combined, collectively enable harmful operations that only become apparent at the final execution step. At the core of \STAC is an automated, closed-loop pipeline that synthesizes executable multi-step tool chains, validates them through in-environment execution, and reverse-engineers stealthy multi-turn prompts that reliably induce agents to execute the verified malicious sequence. Using this framework, we generate and systematically evaluate 483 \STAC cases, featuring 1,352 sets of user-agent-environment interactions and spanning diverse domains, tasks, agent types, and 10 failure modes. Our evaluations show that state-of-the-art LLM agents are highly vulnerable to \STAC, with an average final attack success rate (ASR) of 91.2\% -- exceeding 90\% for all but one of the eight agents evaluated. We further perform defense analysis and find that existing prompt-based defenses provide limited protection. To address this gap, we propose a new reasoning-driven defense prompt that achieves the strongest initial-turn protection, cutting ASR by up to 28.8\%; however, this advantage erodes sharply under adaptive attacks, and an experience-based defense (ToolShield) proves more durable over sustained multi-turn interactions. These results highlight a crucial gap: defending tool-enabled agents requires reasoning over entire action sequences and their cumulative effects, rather than evaluating isolated prompts or responses.
中文标题/摘要
标题：STAC：当无辜工具形成对LLM代理的危险链条
随着LLM向具备工具使用能力的自主代理发展，它们引入了超越传统内容基础LLM安全问题的安全挑战。本文介绍了顺序工具攻击链（STAC），一种新颖的多轮攻击框架，利用代理工具的使用。STAC将看似无害的工具调用串联在一起，但当组合时，集体启用有害操作，这些操作仅在最终执行步骤时显现。STAC的核心是一个自动化的闭环管道，合成可执行的多步骤工具链，通过环境执行验证它们，并逆向工程隐秘的多轮提示，可靠地诱导代理执行经过验证的恶意序列。利用该框架，我们生成并系统评估了483个STAC案例，涉及1,352组用户-代理-环境交互，涵盖多种领域、任务、代理类型和10种失败模式。我们的评估显示，最先进的LLM代理对STAC高度脆弱，平均最终攻击成功率（ASR）为91.2%，除了评估的八个代理中有一个外，其他均超过90%。我们进一步进行防御分析，发现现有的基于提示的防御提供的保护有限。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种新的基于推理的防御提示，达到了最强的初始回合保护，将ASR降低了多达28.8%；然而，在自适应攻击下，这一优势迅速减弱，而基于经验的防御（ToolShield）在持续的多轮交互中表现得更为持久。这些结果突显了一个关键差距：防御工具启用的代理需要对整个行动序列及其累积效果进行推理，而不是评估孤立的提示或响应。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the emerging security challenges posed by large language models (LLMs) as they evolve into autonomous agents capable of tool use, which extends beyond traditional safety concerns. Previous methods primarily focused on content-based defenses, which are inadequate against the novel threat of Sequential Tool Attack Chaining (STAC), where seemingly harmless tool calls can combine to execute harmful operations. This paper proposes STAC, a multi-turn attack framework that automates the synthesis and validation of malicious tool chains, demonstrating a significant vulnerability in state-of-the-art LLM agents with an average attack success rate of 91.2%. The methodology involves generating and evaluating 483 STAC cases across various domains, revealing that existing defenses are insufficient, while a new reasoning-driven defense prompt shows promise but is less effective against adaptive attacks. The findings underscore the necessity for defenses that consider the cumulative effects of entire action sequences rather than isolated prompts.
本研究关注大型语言模型（LLMs）作为自主代理并具备工具使用能力所带来的新兴安全挑战，这些挑战超出了传统内容安全问题的范围。以往的方法主要集中在孤立的提示评估上，未能考虑工具交互的累积效应，而提出的顺序工具攻击链（STAC）框架通过自动化合成和验证多步工具链来克服这一问题，这些工具链在单独使用时看似无害，但结合在一起时可能导致有害结果。本文的贡献在于展示了最先进的LLM代理对STAC的高度脆弱性，在483个案例中平均攻击成功率达到91.2%，同时还提出了一种新的基于推理的防御提示，初始攻击成功率降低了最多28.8%，尽管在适应性攻击下其有效性减弱。该方法论涉及一个闭环管道，在真实环境中生成和评估工具链，强调了需要全面的防御措施，以考虑整个行动序列而非孤立的提示。
Addressing Benchmarking Gaps in Large Language Models for Health and Medicine with Dynamic Red-Teaming
Authors: Jiazhen Pan, Bailiang Jian, Paul Hager, Yundi Zhang, Che Liu, Friederike Jungmann, Hongwei Bran Li, Julian Canisius, Chenyu You, Junde Wu, Jiayuan Zhu, Fenglin Liu, Yuyuan Liu, Niklas Bubeck, Moritz Knolle, Chen, Chen, Christian Wachinger, Zhenyu Gong, Cheng Ouyang, Georgios Kaissis, Benedikt Wiestler, Daniel Rueckert
First: 2025-07-30T08:44:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:41:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to answer health-related questions and support healthcare workflows, yet evidence for their safety still relies heavily on static benchmarks that can rapidly become obsolete or be optimized against. Here we introduce a Dynamic, Automatic, and Systematic (DAS) red-teaming audit framework that continuously stress-tests LLMs for health across four safety-critical axes: robustness, privacy, bias/fairness, and hallucination/factual inaccuracies. Validated against board-certified clinicians with high concordance, a suite of adversarial agents autonomously mutates health-related test cases to uncover vulnerabilities in real time. Applying DAS to 15 proprietary and open-source LLMs revealed a profound gap between high static benchmark performance and low dynamic reliability--the "Benchmarking Gap". Despite median MedQA accuracy exceeding 80\%, 94\% of previously correct answers failed under dynamic robustness testing. This brittleness generalized to the realistic, open-ended HealthBench dataset, where top-tier models exhibited failure rates exceeding 70\% and sharp shifts in model rankings across evaluations, suggesting that high scores on established static benchmarks may reflect superficial memorization. We observed similarly high failure rates across other domains: privacy leaks were elicited in 86\% of scenarios, cognitive-bias priming altered recommendations in 81\% of fairness tests, and hallucination rates exceeded 74\% in widely used models. By converting LLM safety evaluation for health from a static checklist into a living adversarial audit, DAS provides a scalable framework for surfacing latent risks before such systems are deployed in consumer-facing health assistants, clinician-facing tools, and broader healthcare workflows. Code is available at https://github.com/JZPeterPan/DAS-Medical-Red-Teaming-Agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过动态红队解决大型语言模型在健康和医学中的基准测试差距
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地用于回答与健康相关的问题和支持医疗工作流程，但其安全性的证据仍然在很大程度上依赖于快速过时或可优化的静态基准。在这里，我们引入了一种动态、自动和系统化（DAS）红队审计框架，持续对LLMs在健康方面进行压力测试，涵盖四个安全关键轴心：鲁棒性、隐私、公平性/偏见和幻觉/事实不准确性。经过与高一致性的董事会认证临床医生的验证，一套对抗性代理自主变更与健康相关的测试案例，以实时发现漏洞。将DAS应用于15个专有和开源LLMs揭示了高静态基准性能与低动态可靠性之间的深刻差距——“基准测试差距”。尽管中位数MedQA准确率超过80%，但94%的先前正确答案在动态鲁棒性测试中失败。这种脆弱性在现实的开放式HealthBench数据集中普遍存在，顶级模型的失败率超过70%，并且在评估中模型排名发生剧烈变化，表明在既定静态基准上获得的高分可能反映了表面的记忆。我们在其他领域观察到类似的高失败率：在86%的场景中引发了隐私泄露，81%的公平性测试中认知偏见的引导改变了推荐，广泛使用的模型中幻觉率超过74%。通过将LLM的健康安全评估从静态检查表转变为动态对抗审计，DAS提供了一个可扩展的框架，以在这些系统部署到面向消费者的健康助手、面向临床医生的工具和更广泛的医疗工作流程之前，揭示潜在风险。代码可在https://github.com/JZPeterPan/DAS-Medical-Red-Teaming-Agents获取。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the increasing reliance on large language models (LLMs) in healthcare, highlighting the limitations of static benchmarks that may quickly become outdated or manipulated. Previous methods primarily focused on static evaluations, which fail to capture the dynamic nature of real-world applications, leading to a significant 'Benchmarking Gap' where models perform well in controlled tests but poorly in practical scenarios. The proposed Dynamic, Automatic, and Systematic (DAS) red-teaming audit framework continuously evaluates LLMs across critical safety dimensions, revealing vulnerabilities in real time. This methodology was validated against board-certified clinicians and demonstrated that while median MedQA accuracy was over 80%, dynamic robustness testing showed that 94% of previously correct answers failed, with high failure rates in privacy, bias, and hallucination assessments. The findings indicate that existing benchmarks may not accurately reflect model reliability, underscoring the need for a more adaptive evaluation approach in healthcare applications.
本研究关注大型语言模型（LLMs）在医疗保健中的日益依赖，强调静态基准测试的不足，这些测试很快就会过时或被操控。以往的方法主要集中在静态评估上，未能捕捉LLMs的动态脆弱性，导致显著的“基准差距”，即高静态性能与现实世界可靠性之间缺乏相关性。本文提出了一种动态、自动和系统化（DAS）红队审计框架，持续测试LLMs在稳健性、隐私、公平性和事实准确性等关键安全维度上的表现。该方法涉及使用对抗代理自主生成与健康相关的测试案例，揭示尽管MedQA的中位准确率超过80%，但94%的先前正确答案在动态测试中失败，HealthBench数据集上的失败率超过70%。研究结果强调了采用更具适应性的评估方法以确保LLMs在医疗应用中的安全性和可靠性的重要性。
The Joint Effect of Quantization and Sampling Temperature on LLM Safety Alignment: A Factorial Analysis
Authors: Hari Prasad, Ritam Pal
First: 2026-06-28T19:52:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T10:24:21+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 5 Figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern LLM deployments often combine quantization with higher sampling temperatures to reduce cost, latency, or repetition, yet safety evaluations usually treat these as fixed implementation details. We test whether models that are safe at FP16 with greedy decoding remain safe after quantization and stochastic sampling, or whether the two factors amplify each other. We evaluate 8 instruction-tuned models from five families across 3 precisions and 6 temperatures, covering 144 configurations on 7 harmfulness benchmarks and generating about 2.0 million responses, which are scored by a six-judge safety ensemble. Contrary to concerns that low-bit deployment erodes alignment, we find that standard quantization is approximately safety-neutral: for 7 of 8 models, AWQ INT4 keeps attack success within about 1.6 percentage points of FP16 or lowers it, with clear degradation only for SmolLM3-3B (34.5% to 44.1%). However, the larger risk comes from sampling: higher temperatures sharply increase decision instability, with DFR reaching 41.9% at T = 1.0, even when average ASR changes only modestly. The two factors do not compound: our Compound Degradation Index remains sub-additive (-0.071 to +0.018), indicating that quantization partially offsets rather than amplifies temperature-induced degradation. Finally, a per-benchmark breakdown shows that single-benchmark evaluation badly understates risk: several models scoring 0% on AdvBench exceed 80% on ManyHarm. Standard INT4/INT8 quantization can therefore be reasonable for well-aligned models, but safety claims should report multi-sample stability across multiple benchmarks rather than rely on a single benchmark at greedy decoding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：量化与采样温度对大型语言模型安全对齐的联合影响：一个因子分析
现代大型语言模型的部署通常将量化与更高的采样温度结合，以降低成本、延迟或重复性，但安全评估通常将这些视为固定的实施细节。我们测试在FP16下使用贪婪解码安全的模型在量化和随机采样后是否仍然安全，或者这两个因素是否相互放大。我们评估了来自五个家族的8个指令调优模型，涵盖3种精度和6种温度，共144种配置，在7个有害性基准上生成约200万条响应，并由六名评审组成的安全团队进行评分。与低位部署会削弱对齐的担忧相反，我们发现标准量化大致是安全中性的：对于8个模型中的7个，AWQ INT4将攻击成功率保持在FP16的约1.6个百分点以内或降低，只有SmolLM3-3B出现明显退化（34.5%降至44.1%）。然而，较大的风险来自采样：更高的温度显著增加决策不稳定性，在T = 1.0时DFR达到41.9%，即使平均ASR变化仅适度。这两个因素并不相互叠加：我们的复合退化指数保持在亚加性范围内（-0.071至+0.018），表明量化部分抵消而非放大温度引起的退化。最后，逐基准的细分显示，单基准评估严重低估了风险：几个在AdvBench上得分为0%的模型在ManyHarm上超过80%。因此，标准INT4/INT8量化对于对齐良好的模型是合理的，但安全声明应报告多个基准的多样本稳定性，而不是依赖于贪婪解码的单一基准。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the safety alignment of large language models (LLMs) in the context of modern deployments that utilize quantization and higher sampling temperatures, which are often treated as fixed parameters in safety evaluations. Previous methods have not adequately assessed the combined effects of these factors, leading to concerns about potential safety degradation. The proposed approach systematically evaluates the safety of 8 instruction-tuned models across various precisions and temperatures, revealing that standard quantization is generally safety-neutral, while higher sampling temperatures significantly increase decision instability. The paper contributes by demonstrating that quantization does not exacerbate risks associated with temperature increases and emphasizes the importance of multi-benchmark evaluations for accurate safety assessments. The methodology involves a factorial analysis of 144 configurations across 7 harmfulness benchmarks, resulting in findings that support the use of standard quantization for well-aligned models while highlighting the need for comprehensive safety reporting.
本研究探讨了在现代大语言模型（LLMs）部署中，量化与高采样温度结合对安全对齐的影响，这些因素通常在安全评估中被视为固定参数。以往的方法往往忽视了量化与采样温度之间的相互作用，导致对低位部署安全性下降的担忧。提出的方法系统评估了8个指令调优模型在不同精度和温度下的安全性，结果显示标准量化通常是安全中性的，而较高的采样温度显著增加了决策的不稳定性。该论文的贡献在于表明量化并未加剧与高温度相关的风险，并强调多基准评估在准确安全评估中的重要性。该方法论涉及在7个有害性基准上对144种配置进行因子分析，结果表明，对于良好对齐的模型，标准INT4/INT8量化是可接受的，前提是安全评估考虑多样本稳定性。
Minionese: Comprehensive Benchmark and Mechanistic Study of Multilingual LLM Safety
Authors: Chigozirim Ifebi, Brent Kong, Ayushi Mehrotra
First: 2026-07-11T04:13:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-11T04:13:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Safety alignment in large language models remains brittle across languages: prompts reliably refused in English can elicit harmful compliance in non-English and low-resource settings. We introduce \textsc{Minionese}, a multilingual jailbreak benchmark spanning 18 languages, 4 resource tiers, and 4 perturbation types (standard translation, code-switching, transliteration, and translationese), paired with a geometric mechanistic analysis of refusal failure across language tiers. We show that each attack type produces a distinct vulnerability profile: transliteration vulnerability is mediated by script identity, code-switching maintains effectiveness through the lowest-resource tier, and a sharp safety regime transition between Tiers 2 and 3 is consistent across all models. Mechanistically, low-resource jailbreaks succeed by routing harmful content through a geometrically misaligned subspace that projects insufficiently onto the refusal directions, leaving the refusal mechanism intact but untriggered. These findings show that English-only safety evaluations are insufficient; they require accounting for script family, perturbation type, and per-language alignment coverage. The benchmark and analysis code is at https://github.com/Brentkong/Minionese-Comprehensive-Benchmark-and-Mechanistic-Study-of-Multilingual-LLM-Safety.git.
中文标题/摘要
标题：小黄人语：多语言大型语言模型安全性的综合基准和机制研究
大型语言模型的安全对齐在不同语言间仍然脆弱：在英语中可靠拒绝的提示在非英语和低资源环境中可能引发有害的顺从。我们介绍了\textsc{Minionese}，这是一个涵盖18种语言、4个资源层级和4种扰动类型（标准翻译、代码切换、音译和翻译语）的多语言越狱基准，并结合了对不同语言层级拒绝失败的几何机制分析。我们展示了每种攻击类型产生独特的脆弱性特征：音译脆弱性受脚本身份的影响，代码切换在最低资源层级中保持有效，而第2层和第3层之间的安全机制转变在所有模型中是一致的。从机制上讲，低资源越狱通过将有害内容引导到几何上不对齐的子空间中成功，该子空间在拒绝方向上投影不足，保持拒绝机制完好但未被触发。这些发现表明，仅依赖英语的安全评估是不够的；它们需要考虑脚本家族、扰动类型和每种语言的对齐覆盖。基准和分析代码可在https://github.com/Brentkong/Minionese-Comprehensive-Benchmark-and-Mechanistic-Study-of-Multilingual-LLM-Safety.git找到。

Previous methods primarily focused on English evaluations, failing to account for the nuances of multilingual environments, which resulted in vulnerabilities being overlooked. The proposed approach, Minionese, introduces a comprehensive multilingual jailbreak benchmark that spans 18 languages and various resource tiers, coupled with a mechanistic analysis of refusal failures. This methodology reveals distinct vulnerability profiles for different attack types and highlights the inadequacy of English-only safety assessments. The findings indicate that the proposed benchmark effectively identifies vulnerabilities in multilingual settings, demonstrating that safety evaluations must consider language-specific factors to enhance model robustness.

Mechanistic Interpretability of LLM Jailbreaks via Internal Attribution Graphs
Authors: Anupam Wagle, Ifrat Ikhtear Uddin, Chaowei Zhang, Longwei Wang
First: 2026-07-08T20:31:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T20:31:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) exhibit remarkable capabilities but remain highly vulnerable to adversarial prompts and jailbreak attacks. Existing approaches primarily analyze these failures through input-output behaviors or attribution methods, offering limited insight into how adversarial perturbations alter the model's internal reasoning. Consequently, the mechanisms underlying unsafe or incorrect behaviors remain poorly understood. We introduce a mechanistic framework for diagnosing LLM vulnerabilities using paired internal computation graphs, which represent prompt-specific inference as structured causal interactions among latent features. By constructing and aligning computation graphs for clean and attacked prompts, we reveal that adversarial attacks induce systematic transformations of internal reasoning, including suppression of safety-relevant components, emergence of attack-specific features, and rerouting of computation paths. Building on this representation, we propose a unified framework that (i) decomposes computation into invariant, suppressed, and emergent structures, (ii) identifies recurring vulnerability motifs associated with failure modes, and (iii) performs causal interventions on nodes, paths, and subgraphs to directly evaluate their contributions to attack success. This enables a transition from descriptive attribution to causal diagnosis of model failures. Experiments across multiple open-source LLMs and diverse adversarial and jailbreak benchmarks demonstrate that structural deviations in internal computation graphs strongly correlate with unsafe behaviors. Furthermore, targeted interventions on identified vulnerability motifs improve model robustness, establishing internal computation graphs as a principled foundation for understanding, diagnosing, and mitigating LLM vulnerabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过内部归因图对LLM越狱的机制性可解释性
大型语言模型（LLMs）展现出显著的能力，但仍然对对抗性提示和越狱攻击高度脆弱。现有方法主要通过输入输出行为或归因方法分析这些失败，提供的见解有限，无法揭示对抗性扰动如何改变模型的内部推理。因此，导致不安全或不正确行为的机制仍然不清楚。我们引入了一种机制框架，通过配对的内部计算图来诊断LLM的脆弱性，这些图将特定提示的推理表示为潜在特征之间的结构因果交互。通过构建和对齐干净和攻击提示的计算图，我们揭示了对抗攻击引发的内部推理的系统性转变，包括安全相关组件的抑制、攻击特定特征的出现和计算路径的重新引导。在此表示的基础上，我们提出了一个统一框架，(i) 将计算分解为不变、抑制和新兴结构，(ii) 识别与失败模式相关的重复脆弱性特征，(iii) 对节点、路径和子图进行因果干预，以直接评估它们对攻击成功的贡献。这使得从描述性归因转向模型失败的因果诊断成为可能。针对多个开源LLM和多样的对抗性及越狱基准的实验表明，内部计算图的结构偏差与不安全行为强烈相关。此外，对识别出的脆弱性特征进行有针对性的干预提高了模型的鲁棒性，确立了内部计算图作为理解、诊断和减轻LLM脆弱性的原则基础。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the vulnerabilities of large language models (LLMs) to adversarial prompts and jailbreak attacks, which have been inadequately analyzed by existing methods that focus on input-output behaviors or attribution techniques. These traditional approaches fail to provide a comprehensive understanding of how adversarial perturbations affect the model's internal reasoning. The proposed method introduces a mechanistic framework utilizing paired internal computation graphs to diagnose LLM vulnerabilities, revealing systematic transformations in internal reasoning due to adversarial attacks. The paper contributes by offering a unified framework that decomposes computation into invariant, suppressed, and emergent structures, identifies recurring vulnerability motifs, and allows for causal interventions to evaluate their impact on attack success. Experiments demonstrate that structural deviations in internal computation graphs correlate with unsafe behaviors, and targeted interventions on vulnerability motifs enhance model robustness, supporting the goal of improving LLM safety and interpretability.

Predicting LLM Safety Before Release by Simulating Deployment
Authors: Marcus Williams, Hannah Sheahan, Cameron Raymond, Tomek Korbak, Deng Pan, Peilin Yang, Leon Maksin, Ningyi Xie, Phillip Guo, Ian Kivlichan, Micah Carroll
First: 2026-07-08T09:17:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T09:17:47+00:00
Comments: 31 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pre-deployment safety evaluations aim to inform the downstream risks of releasing a new AI model. Yet most evaluations provide limited evidence about how often undesired model behavior will occur in deployment: they generally have insufficient coverage, are unrepresentative, and are generally recognizable as tests. To address these concerns, we study a simple way to simulate a model deployment: starting from de-identified conversations from a previous model deployment, we hold fixed the initial conversation prefix and regenerate the next response using a candidate model. The resulting responses can then both be audited for novel misalignments and used to estimate the prevalence of model misbehavior before deployment. We evaluate deployment simulation across four GPT-5-series deployments, using registered, outcome-blinded predictions for GPT-5.4 and retrospective analyses of three earlier releases. We find that deployment simulation produces informative estimates of post-deployment misbehavior rates and outperforms baselines based on adversarially selected production data; its evaluation-awareness point estimates were also much closer to production traffic than those from traditional evaluations. We also identify the realism of tool resampling as a central challenge for further improving predictions and share results suggesting that this challenge is surmountable even in complex tool-use settings. Finally, we show that deployment simulation can be seeded from public chat datasets and remain informative about production misbehavior rates, suggesting a path for external researchers to run deployment-grounded evaluations without access to private production logs. Overall, deployment simulation helps evaluators forecast how language models will behave in the real world and supports more quantitative assessment of deployment risk.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过模拟部署预测 LLM 发布前的安全性
发布前的安全评估旨在告知发布新 AI 模型的下游风险。然而，大多数评估提供的证据有限，无法说明在部署中不希望的模型行为发生的频率：它们通常覆盖不足、代表性差，并且通常被识别为测试。为了解决这些问题，我们研究了一种简单的模拟模型部署的方法：从先前模型部署的去标识化对话开始，固定初始对话前缀，并使用候选模型重新生成下一个响应。生成的响应可以用于审计新出现的不一致性，并用于在部署前估计模型不当行为的普遍性。我们在四个 GPT-5 系列部署中评估了部署模拟，使用了对 GPT-5.4 的注册、结果盲预测和对三个早期发布的回顾性分析。我们发现，部署模拟产生了有关部署后不当行为率的信息性估计，并且优于基于对抗性选择的生产数据的基线；其评估意识点估计也比传统评估更接近生产流量。我们还确定了工具重采样的现实性作为进一步改善预测的核心挑战，并分享了结果，表明即使在复杂的工具使用环境中，这一挑战也是可以克服的。最后，我们展示了部署模拟可以从公共聊天数据集中播种，并保持对生产不当行为率的信息性，这为外部研究人员在没有访问私有生产日志的情况下进行基于部署的评估提供了一条路径。总体而言，部署模拟帮助评估者预测语言模型在现实世界中的行为，并支持对部署风险的更定量评估。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of pre-deployment safety evaluations for AI models, which often fail to provide comprehensive insights into potential misbehavior during actual deployment. Previous methods typically lack sufficient coverage and representativeness, leading to unreliable assessments. This paper proposes a novel approach that simulates model deployment by using de-identified conversations from past deployments to generate responses with a candidate model, allowing for better auditing of misalignments and estimation of misbehavior prevalence. The methodology was tested across four GPT-5-series deployments, revealing that deployment simulation yields more accurate estimates of post-deployment misbehavior rates compared to traditional evaluations. The findings indicate that this method not only enhances the understanding of model behavior in real-world scenarios but also offers a viable option for external researchers to conduct evaluations without access to private data.
本研究解决了人工智能模型预发布安全评估的局限性，这些评估通常缺乏足够的覆盖面和代表性，导致对实际应用中不良行为的预测不可靠。传统方法通常依赖于对抗性选择的生产数据，这些数据无法准确反映部署场景。所提出的方法通过使用先前部署的去标识化对话来模拟模型部署，从而生成候选模型的响应，这使得对不一致性进行更好的审计和对不当行为发生率的估计成为可能。该方法的动机明确，因为它提高了对模型发布后行为的预测准确性。研究在四个GPT-5系列部署中评估了这种模拟，发现其提供的误行为率估计比传统评估更具信息量，从而支持了在发布前改善人工智能模型安全评估的目标。
Operational Reframing and Approval-Framed Delegation in Multi-Agent LLM Safety
Authors: Lifei Liu, Haoran Yu, Xiaochong Jiang, Su Wang, Pin Qian, Yihang Chen
First: 2026-07-08T07:31:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T07:31:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety evaluations of multi-agent LLM systems often compare a direct prompt with a planner-executor pipeline and report the difference as a single "pipeline effect." We argue that this aggregate is difficult to interpret because it conflates three mechanisms: harmful intent may be reframed as plausible operational work, the planner may refuse or transform the request, and the executor may act under delegation prompts implying prior approval. To separate these factors, we introduce a five-condition controlled contrast design, evaluated on 30 synthetic harmful scenarios and an exploratory external validation set from four agent-safety benchmarks using LLM-judged compliance. Our results show that aggregate pipeline safety is not a stable architectural property. Operational reframing is the most portable risk signal, increasing compliance for GPT, Gemini, and DeepSeek across both scenario sets, while Claude is comparatively resistant. Planner behavior can offset this risk mainly through refusal; however, when the planner produces executable steps, the executor may become more compliant than under the direct operational baseline. Approval-framed delegation is sensitive to prompt design, model pairing, and scenario source, and a skeptical executor prompt sharply reduces compliance. Raw-direct model rankings can also mispredict deployed planner-executor behavior. Gemini is safest under raw direct prompts in the primary set yet shows the largest amplification with a Claude planner, rising from 8.9 percent to 38.9 percent compliance. GPTs near-zero aggregate pipeline effect instead hides a reframing increase canceled by planner refusal. These findings suggest that multi-agent safety evaluations should report reframing, planner behavior, delegation framing, and model pairing separately before attributing failures to architecture itself.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多智能体LLM安全中的操作重构与批准框架委托
多智能体LLM系统的安全评估通常将直接提示与规划-执行管道进行比较，并将差异报告为单一的“管道效应”。我们认为，这种汇总难以解释，因为它混淆了三种机制：有害意图可能被重构为合理的操作工作，规划者可能拒绝或转变请求，执行者可能在暗示先前批准的委托提示下行动。为了分离这些因素，我们引入了一个五条件的对照设计，在30个合成有害场景和来自四个代理安全基准的探索性外部验证集上进行评估，使用LLM判断的合规性。我们的结果表明，汇总管道安全并不是一个稳定的架构属性。操作重构是最具可移植性的风险信号，在两个场景集上提高了GPT、Gemini和DeepSeek的合规性，而Claude则相对抵抗。规划者行为主要通过拒绝来抵消这一风险；然而，当规划者产生可执行步骤时，执行者的合规性可能比直接操作基线更高。批准框架委托对提示设计、模型配对和场景来源敏感，而怀疑的执行者提示会显著降低合规性。原始直接模型排名也可能错误预测已部署的规划-执行行为。在主要集中的原始直接提示下，Gemini是最安全的，但在Claude规划者下显示出最大的放大，从8.9%上升到38.9%的合规性。GPT的近零汇总管道效应则掩盖了被规划者拒绝抵消的重构增加。这些发现表明，多智能体安全评估应分别报告重构、规划者行为、委托框架和模型配对，然后再将失败归因于架构本身。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the complexities in safety evaluations of multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems, which have traditionally aggregated results into a single 'pipeline effect,' obscuring the underlying mechanisms at play. Previous methods have struggled to disentangle the effects of harmful intent, planner behavior, and executor compliance, leading to misinterpretations of safety performance. The proposed approach introduces a controlled contrast design with five conditions to better isolate these factors, demonstrating that operational reframing is a significant risk signal and that planner behavior can influence executor compliance. The methodology involves evaluating 30 synthetic harmful scenarios and an external validation set across four agent-safety benchmarks, revealing that compliance varies significantly based on prompt design and model pairing. The findings indicate that multi-agent safety evaluations should separately report these factors to provide a clearer understanding of safety dynamics, challenging the notion that aggregate pipeline safety is a stable property.

DT-Guard: Intent-Driven Reasoning-Active Training for Reasoning-Free LLM Safety Guardrail
Authors: He Liu, Changtao Miao, Xinjie Yang, Tianle Song, Yin Wu, Junchi Chen, Bintao He, Xinyuan Zhang, Bo Zhang, Shi Yan, Wei Lu, Wei Wang, Danyang Xu, Jiansheng Cai, Zhe Li
First: 2026-07-07T14:25:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T14:25:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models deployed in open-world applications require safety guardrails that are both robust to complex risks and efficient enough for low-latency runtime moderation. Existing guardrails face a practical trade-off between lightweight classification-based models, which are efficient but often struggle with concealed intent, ambiguous semantics, and borderline safety decisions, and reasoning-based guards, which improve judgment quality but introduce additional token generation and inference latency. We present DT-Guard, a content safety guardrail model based on a Reasoning-Active Training, Reasoning-Free Inference paradigm. The key idea is to use reasoning supervision during training while emitting only structured safety labels at inference time. DT-Guard formulates safety judgment as a progressive decision process, Intent - Category - Safety, and constructs an intent-driven dataset with intent labels, risk categories, safety labels, and structured reasoning trajectories. To further improve hard-case robustness, we propose Rollout-Guided Progressive Hard-Case Optimization (RG-PHO), which uses multi-rollout consistency to identify stably mastered, persistently failed, and preference-unstable samples, and applies targeted supervised and preference optimization accordingly. At inference time, DT-Guard directly generates structured labels without explicit reasoning traces, preserving deployment efficiency. Experiments on prompt-side and response-side safety benchmarks show that DT-Guard achieves average F1 scores of 0.886 and 0.870, respectively. With only a 4B backbone, it reaches a dual-side average F1 of 0.878, outperforming strong 8B guardrail baselines. These results demonstrate that reasoning supervision can be effectively internalized into low-latency safety discrimination.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DT-Guard：基于意图驱动推理主动训练的无推理大型语言模型安全护栏
在开放世界应用中部署的大型语言模型需要既能应对复杂风险又能高效低延迟的安全护栏。现有护栏在轻量级基于分类的模型（高效但常常难以处理隐含意图、模糊语义和边界安全决策）与基于推理的护栏（提高判断质量但引入额外的令牌生成和推理延迟）之间面临实际权衡。我们提出了DT-Guard，一种基于推理主动训练、无推理推断范式的内容安全护栏模型。其关键思想是在训练期间使用推理监督，而在推断时仅发出结构化安全标签。DT-Guard将安全判断公式化为一个渐进决策过程：意图 - 类别 - 安全，并构建了一个带有意图标签、风险类别、安全标签和结构化推理轨迹的意图驱动数据集。为了进一步提高难例的鲁棒性，我们提出了回滚引导渐进难例优化（RG-PHO），利用多回滚一致性识别稳定掌握、持续失败和偏好不稳定的样本，并相应地应用针对性的监督和偏好优化。在推断时，DT-Guard直接生成结构化标签，而无需显式推理痕迹，从而保持部署效率。在提示端和响应端安全基准上的实验表明，DT-Guard分别达到了平均F1分数0.886和0.870。仅使用4B主干，它的双侧平均F1达到了0.878，超越了强大的8B护栏基线。这些结果表明，推理监督可以有效地内化为低延迟的安全判别。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the need for effective safety guardrails in large language models (LLMs) used in open-world applications, highlighting the limitations of existing methods that either sacrifice efficiency for accuracy or vice versa. Previous approaches often struggle with concealed intent and ambiguous semantics, leading to suboptimal safety decisions. The proposed DT-Guard model introduces a Reasoning-Active Training and Reasoning-Free Inference paradigm, which allows for reasoning supervision during training while maintaining efficiency during inference by generating structured safety labels without explicit reasoning traces. This method enhances robustness against hard cases through Rollout-Guided Progressive Hard-Case Optimization, which targets specific sample weaknesses. Chasing Moving Targets with Online Self-Play Reinforcement Learning for Safer Language Models
Authors: Mickel Liu, Liwei Jiang, Yancheng Liang, Simon Shaolei Du, Yejin Choi, Tim Althoff, Natasha Jaques
Venue: ICML 2026 Poster
First: 2025-06-09T06:35:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T07:31:35+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026 Poster
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Conventional large language model (LLM) safety alignment relies on a reactive, disjoint loop: attackers exploit a static model, then defenders patch exposed vulnerabilities. This sequential setup leads to attackers overfitting obsolete exploits while defenders perpetually lag behind emerging threats. To address this, we introduce Self-RedTeam, the first fully online self-play multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) algorithm that continuously co-evolves attacker and defender for robust safety alignment. A single policy self-plays as both attacker and defender, generating adversarial prompts and defending against them, with a reward model adjudicating outcomes. Each role uses hidden chain-of-thought for strategic planning. Grounded in two-player zero-sum game theory, we establish a theoretical safety guarantee: if the game converges to Nash Equilibrium, the defender produces safe responses against any adversarial input. Empirically, Self-RedTeam generalizes across five models from the Llama and Qwen families, uncovering more diverse attacks (+17.80% SBERT) and improving safety of RLHF-trained models by up to 95% across 14 benchmarks. Our work motivates a shift from reactive patching to proactive co-evolution, enabling LLM safety self-improvement via online self-play MARL. Link to code: https://github.com/mickelliu/selfplay-redteaming

标题：通过在线自我对弈强化学习追踪移动目标以实现更安全的语言模型
传统的大型语言模型（LLM）安全对齐依赖于反应式的、不连贯的循环：攻击者利用静态模型，防御者修补暴露的漏洞。这种顺序设置导致攻击者过度拟合过时的攻击，而防御者则始终滞后于新兴威胁。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了Self-RedTeam，这是第一个完全在线自我对弈的多智能体强化学习（MARL）算法，持续共同进化攻击者和防御者以实现稳健的安全对齐。单一策略同时扮演攻击者和防御者，生成对抗性提示并进行防御，奖励模型裁定结果。每个角色使用隐含的思维链进行战略规划。基于双人零和博弈理论，我们建立了理论安全保证：如果游戏收敛到纳什均衡，防御者将对任何对抗性输入产生安全响应。在实证上，Self-RedTeam在Llama和Qwen家族的五个模型中具有广泛的泛化能力，发现了更多样化的攻击（+17.80% SBERT），并在14个基准测试中将RLHF训练模型的安全性提高了多达95%。我们的工作激励了从反应式修补到主动共同进化的转变，使LLM安全性通过在线自我对弈MARL实现自我改进。

Behind the Refusal: Determining Guardrail Activation via Behavioral Monitoring
Authors: William Hackett, Peter Garraghan
First: 2026-07-02T12:59:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T12:59:28+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 13 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As Large Language Models (LLMs) and agentic systems become integrated into real-world applications, ensuring their safety and security is critical. Guardrail systems that detect and block malicious instructions sent to and from an LLM are an essential component of AI security. However, researchers conducting black-box adversarial emulation against production AI systems often struggle to determine whether a guardrail block or an LLM rejection has occurred. This distinction is important because the techniques used to bypass guardrails can differ substantially from those used to bypass LLM safety alignment, and has a material impact on attack technique selection and optimization. We propose the first black-box guardrail reconnaissance methodology, which detects the presence of a guardrail within a target AI system through behavioral monitoring of HTTP, lexical, and timing signals, assuming only black-box access and zero prior knowledge of the guardrail or AI system. Experiments demonstrate that our approach detects guardrail presence with 100% accuracy, with statistically significant behavioral separation between benign and malicious interactions (q < 0.001). Our approach further identifies the content categories a guardrail is designed to block, and distinguishes guardrail blocks from LLM rejection on unseen prompts with an average F1 score of 98%.
中文标题/摘要

Optimization Algorithms for Joint OFDM Waveform Design and RIS Configuration in 6G Networks: From Convex Relaxation to Foundation Models
Authors: Ahmet Kaplan
First: 2026-06-30T08:34:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T08:34:46+00:00
Comments: 22 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Joint OFDM-RIS optimization for 6G is a mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) problem covering sum-rate maximization, energy efficiency, max-min fairness, and peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR)-constrained objectives. Seventy-eight joint OFDM-RIS optimization works published between 2021 and 2026 are surveyed. No standardized benchmark exists, and cross-paper comparisons remain infeasible. This survey classifies these works into four paradigms: (I) model-based convex relaxation, (II) heuristic and metaheuristic search, (III) deep reinforcement and unsupervised learning, and (IV) emerging methods including foundation models (FM), diffusion-based generative AI, and quantum optimization. A literature synthesis of self-reported benchmarks shows that ML-based methods (Paradigm~III) report 95-99\% of model-based spectral efficiency at 10^2-10^4 x faster per-inference runtime (method-pair dependent; literature values are self-reported and exclude ML pre-training cost). A companion tutorial benchmark at N=16, N=64, and N=128 reveals a critical scaling property: GPU-based neural network inference (DDQN, PPO, graph neural network (GNN), unsupervised DL) is N-invariant, with identical runtime at N=16 and N=128, while iterative solvers (AO+SCA, PSO) scale polynomially. Energy efficiency (P2) and PAPR-constrained (P4) benchmarks are deferred to future work with standardized power models and waveform generators. Six open challenges emerge from the synthesis: the cross-paradigm benchmark deficit, real-world hardware-constrained deployment, joint waveform-RIS optimization for doubly-dispersive channels, multi-objective PAPR trade-offs, LLM safety in live network control, and diminishing returns of standalone heuristics. We specify requirements for a standardized benchmark. This study serves as a roadmap for researchers and practitioners working on joint OFDM-RIS optimization in 6G networks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：6G网络中联合OFDM波形设计和RIS配置的优化算法：从凸松弛到基础模型
6G中的联合OFDM-RIS优化是一个混合整数非线性规划（MINLP）问题，涵盖了总速率最大化、能量效率、最大-最小公平性和峰均功率比（PAPR）约束目标。调查了2021年至2026年间发表的78项联合OFDM-RIS优化工作。没有标准化基准，跨论文比较仍然不可行。本调查将这些工作分为四种范式：（I）基于模型的凸松弛，（II）启发式和元启发式搜索，（III）深度强化和无监督学习，以及（IV）新兴方法，包括基础模型（FM）、基于扩散的生成AI和量子优化。自报告基准的文献综合表明，基于机器学习的方法（范式III）在每次推理运行时间上以10^2-10^4倍的速度报告95-99%的基于模型的谱效率（方法对依赖；文献值为自报告并不包括机器学习预训练成本）。在N=16、N=64和N=128的伴随教程基准中揭示了一个关键的缩放特性：基于GPU的神经网络推理（DDQN、PPO、图神经网络（GNN）、无监督深度学习）是N不变的，在N=16和N=128时具有相同的运行时间，而迭代求解器（AO+SCA、PSO）则呈多项式缩放。能量效率（P2）和PAPR约束（P4）基准推迟到未来工作，采用标准化功率模型和波形生成器。从综合中出现六个开放挑战：跨范式基准缺口、现实世界硬件约束部署、双散射信道的联合波形-RIS优化、多目标PAPR权衡、LLM在实时网络控制中的安全性，以及独立启发式的收益递减。我们为标准化基准指定了要求。本研究为在6G网络中从事联合OFDM-RIS优化的研究人员和实践者提供了路线图。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the optimization of joint OFDM-RIS in 6G networks, highlighting the lack of standardized benchmarks and the challenges in cross-paper comparisons. Previous methods include model-based convex relaxation, heuristic searches, and machine learning approaches, which face issues such as inefficiency and scalability. The proposed approach introduces a comprehensive survey of 78 optimization works, categorizing them into four paradigms and demonstrating that machine learning methods can achieve 95-99% of the spectral efficiency of model-based methods while being significantly faster. This paper contributes by identifying six open challenges in the field and proposing a roadmap for future research, emphasizing the need for standardized benchmarks. The methodology involves a literature synthesis and comparative analysis of existing techniques, revealing critical insights into performance scaling and efficiency in joint OFDM-RIS optimization tasks.
本研究关注6G网络中联合OFDM-RIS的优化，强调缺乏标准化基准和跨论文比较的挑战。以往的方法包括基于模型的凸松弛、启发式搜索和机器学习方法，但都面临可扩展性和效率等限制。本文对78项优化工作进行了全面调查，将其分类为四种范式，并证明机器学习方法在推理时间上显著更快，同时实现了相似的谱效率。研究方法包括文献综合和教程基准，揭示了各种算法的关键缩放特性。研究结果表明，尽管机器学习方法展现出潜力，但在实现能效和解决多目标权衡方面仍存在开放挑战，为该领域未来的研究提供了路线图。
Robust Harmful Features Under Jailbreak Attacks: Mechanistic Evidence from Attention Head Specialization in Large Language Models
Authors: Yanchen Yin, Dongqi Han, Linghui Li
Venue: ICML 2026 Oral presentation
First: 2026-06-26T14:51:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T15:00:34+00:00
Comments: 33 pages, 19 figures. Accepted at ICML 2026 as an Oral presentation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Jailbreak attacks bypass LLM safety alignment, yet their mechanisms remain poorly understood. We provide evidence that attacks do not comprehensively eliminate safety features, but instead selectively suppress specific attention heads. We identify two functionally differentiated types: Adversarially Compromised Heads (ACHs) concentrated in early layers, which are suppressed under attacks, and Safety-Aligned Heads (SAHs) in mid-layers, which maintain robust activations even when attacks succeed. Ablation studies support the causal role of ACHs and the contribution of SAHs to robust activations: suppressing a small number of ACHs is sufficient to induce jailbreak-like behavior on normally refused inputs, while removing SAHs substantially weakens mid-layer safety activations. Token-level attribution further shows that ACH suppression is driven specifically by attack-template tokens, providing a mechanistic account of why attacks can bypass refusal decisions through ACH suppression while leaving internal safety signals sustained by SAHs -- a phenomenon we term Robust Harmful Features. To validate the practical significance of this robustness, we show that simply reading these persistent activations -- without any training -- yields competitive aggregate detection performance with strong adversarial robustness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：监狱攻击下的稳健有害特征：来自大型语言模型注意力头专业化的机制证据
监狱攻击绕过了大型语言模型的安全对齐，但其机制仍然不甚了解。我们提供证据表明，攻击并未全面消除安全特征，而是选择性地抑制特定的注意力头。我们识别出两种功能上不同的类型：集中在早期层的对抗性妥协头（ACHs），在攻击下被抑制；以及在中层的安全对齐头（SAHs），即使在攻击成功时也能保持稳健的激活。消融研究支持ACHs的因果作用以及SAHs对稳健激活的贡献：抑制少量ACHs足以在通常拒绝的输入上诱发类似监狱攻击的行为，而移除SAHs则显著削弱中层的安全激活。标记级归因进一步表明，ACH抑制是由攻击模板标记特定驱动的，提供了攻击为何能够通过ACH抑制绕过拒绝决策而保持SAHs支持的内部安全信号的机制性解释——这一现象我们称之为稳健有害特征。为了验证这种稳健性的实际意义，我们展示了仅仅读取这些持续的激活——无需任何训练——就能在强对抗稳健性下获得竞争性的整体检测性能。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the challenge of jailbreak attacks on large language models (LLMs), which compromise safety alignment mechanisms. Previous methods have not fully elucidated how these attacks operate, often overlooking the selective suppression of specific attention heads rather than a complete failure of safety features. The proposed approach identifies two types of attention heads: Adversarially Compromised Heads (ACHs), which are suppressed during attacks, and Safety-Aligned Heads (SAHs), which maintain their functionality. This differentiation is well-motivated as it provides a clearer understanding of the mechanisms behind jailbreak attacks. The methodology involves ablation studies and token-level attribution to demonstrate that suppressing ACHs can lead to jailbreak behavior, while SAHs contribute to maintaining safety activations. The findings indicate that simply leveraging the persistent activations of SAHs can yield competitive detection performance against jailbreak attempts, thus supporting the goal of enhancing LLM robustness against such attacks.
本研究解决了对大型语言模型（LLMs）中的越狱攻击机制理解不足的问题，这些攻击能够绕过安全对齐。以往的方法未能全面阐明这些攻击的运作方式，常常忽视了特定注意力头的选择性抑制，而不是安全特征的完全失效。本文提出了一种新方法，通过识别两种类型的注意力头：对抗性受损头（ACHs）和安全对齐头（SAHs），这两者在模型对攻击的反应中发挥着不同的作用。研究方法包括消融研究和标记级归因，表明抑制ACHs会导致类似越狱的行为，而SAHs则保持安全激活。研究结果显示，读取SAHs的持久激活可以在抵御攻击方面实现竞争性的检测性能，从而支持增强LLM对越狱尝试的鲁棒性的目标。
CASE-Bench: Context-Aware SafEty Benchmark for Large Language Models
Authors: Guangzhi Sun, Xiao Zhan, Shutong Feng, Philip C. Woodland, Jose Such
Venue: ICML 2025
First: 2025-01-24T21:55:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-28T22:17:10+00:00
Comments: 24 pages. This paper has been accepted at ICML 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Aligning large language models (LLMs) with human values is essential for their safe deployment and widespread adoption. Current LLM safety benchmarks often focus solely on the refusal of individual problematic queries, which overlooks the importance of the context where the query occurs and may cause undesired refusal of queries under safe contexts that diminish user experience. Addressing this gap, we introduce CASE-Bench, a Context-Aware SafEty Benchmark that integrates context into safety assessments of LLMs. CASE-Bench assigns distinct, formally described contexts to categorized queries based on Contextual Integrity theory. Additionally, in contrast to previous studies which mainly rely on majority voting from just a few annotators, we recruited a sufficient number of annotators necessary to ensure the detection of statistically significant differences among the experimental conditions based on power analysis. Our extensive analysis using CASE-Bench on various open-source and commercial LLMs reveals a substantial and significant influence of context on human judgments (p<0.0001 from a z-test), underscoring the necessity of context in safety evaluations. We also identify notable mismatches between human judgments and LLM responses, particularly in commercial models within safe contexts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CASE-Bench：面向上下文的安全基准测试用于大型语言模型
将大型语言模型（LLMs）与人类价值观对齐对于其安全部署和广泛采用至关重要。目前的LLM安全基准测试通常仅关注拒绝个别有问题的查询，这忽视了查询发生的上下文的重要性，可能导致在安全上下文中不必要地拒绝查询，从而降低用户体验。为了解决这一问题，我们引入了CASE-Bench，一个将上下文整合到LLM安全评估中的面向上下文的安全基准测试。CASE-Bench根据上下文完整性理论为分类查询分配独特的、正式描述的上下文。此外，与以往主要依赖少数注释者的多数投票的研究不同，我们招募了足够数量的注释者，以确保根据功效分析检测实验条件之间的统计显著差异。我们对各种开源和商业LLM使用CASE-Bench进行的广泛分析揭示了上下文对人类判断的显著影响（p<0.0001，来自z检验），强调了上下文在安全评估中的必要性。我们还发现人类判断与LLM响应之间存在显著不匹配，特别是在安全上下文中的商业模型。

SCARCE: Scalable Cascade Analysis for Rare-event Characterisation via Embeddings
Authors: Yingjie Wang, Yi Dong, Edmund Lau, Jie Meng, Taylor T Johnson, Xiaowei Huang
First: 2026-06-28T21:59:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-28T21:59:31+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 11 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rare events govern the safety profile of modern AI systems, yet their probabilities are extremely difficult to estimate: direct Monte Carlo requires prohibitive sample budgets. Subset Simulation (SS) addresses this by decomposing a rare-event probability into moderate conditional probabilities over nested intermediate events. However, classical SS requires a handcrafted scalar performance function whose sublevel sets define those events, demanding detailed knowledge of the failure geometry and limiting transfer to new domains. We propose SCARCE (Scalable Cascade Analysis for Rare-event Characterisation via Embeddings), which replaces the performance function with learned latent representations and geometric rulers that score proximity to failure regions. Adaptive thresholding constructs nested intermediate events directly from data. We formalise SCARCE through a non-negative supermartingale, yielding a high-probability upper envelope that remains valid under early stopping. On MNIST misclassification, where dense Monte Carlo provides ground truth, SCARCE achieves approximately 400--500 times lower mean absolute error than grid-searched traditional SS while eliminating systematic over-counting. We then study PAIR-style LLM jailbreaks under a fleet-level threat model with adversarial fraction $η$. On Llama-Guard-3-8B hidden states, a PCA-based ruler attains 2.6% mean relative error for $η\geq 10^{-3}$ against finite-sample references whose average bootstrap relative half-width is 27.9%, and transfers to a GCG-style corpus with 2.93% relative error after recalibration. A directional criterion $\mathrm{KL}(p_{\mathrm{good}}\,\|\,p_{\mathrm{bad}})$ ranks rulers consistently with estimation error (Spearman $ρ=0.83$).
中文标题/摘要
标题：SCARCE：通过嵌入进行稀有事件特征化的可扩展级联分析
稀有事件主导现代人工智能系统的安全特性，但其概率极难估计：直接的蒙特卡洛方法需要巨大的样本预算。子集模拟（SS）通过将稀有事件概率分解为嵌套中间事件的适度条件概率来解决这个问题。然而，经典的SS需要手工制作的标量性能函数，其子水平集定义这些事件，要求对失效几何形状有详细了解，并限制了向新领域的转移。我们提出了SCARCE（通过嵌入进行稀有事件特征化的可扩展级联分析），用学习的潜在表示和几何尺代替性能函数，以评分接近失效区域。自适应阈值直接从数据构建嵌套中间事件。我们通过非负超鞅形式化SCARCE，产生一个在早期停止下仍然有效的高概率上包络。在MNIST错误分类中，稠密的蒙特卡洛提供了真实值，SCARCE的平均绝对误差比网格搜索的传统SS低约400-500倍，同时消除了系统性过度计数。然后，我们在具有对抗比例$η$的舰队级威胁模型下研究PAIR风格的LLM越狱。在Llama-Guard-3-8B隐藏状态上，基于PCA的尺在$η\geq 10^{-3}$时对有限样本参考的平均相对误差达到2.6%，其平均自助法相对半宽度为27.9%，并在重新校准后转移到GCG风格的语料库，具有2.93%的相对误差。方向标准$\mathrm{KL}(p_{\mathrm{good}}\,\|\,p_{\mathrm{bad}})$与估计误差一致地对尺进行排名（斯皮尔曼$ρ=0.83$）。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of estimating rare event probabilities in AI systems, which are critical for assessing safety but difficult to quantify using traditional methods like Monte Carlo simulations due to high sample requirements. Existing approaches, such as Subset Simulation (SS), rely on handcrafted performance functions that necessitate extensive knowledge of failure geometries, limiting their applicability to new domains. The proposed method, SCARCE, innovatively utilizes learned latent representations and geometric rulers to define failure regions, allowing for adaptive thresholding that directly constructs intermediate events from data. This approach is well-motivated as it overcomes the limitations of classical SS, resulting in significant improvements in performance. In experiments, SCARCE demonstrated approximately 400-500 times lower mean absolute error on MNIST misclassification compared to traditional SS, and effectively analyzed PAIR-style LLM jailbreaks, achieving a mean relative error of 2.6% for adversarial fractions, thus supporting its goals of accurate rare event characterization.
本研究解决了在人工智能系统中估计稀有事件概率的挑战，这对于评估安全性至关重要，但由于传统方法（如蒙特卡洛和子集模拟（SS））的局限性而难以量化。现有的SS方法需要手工制作的性能函数，这限制了它们在新领域的适用性，并依赖于详细的故障几何知识。提出的方法SCARCE创新性地利用学习的潜在表示和自适应阈值，从数据中直接构建中间事件，从而克服了经典SS的局限性。本文通过非负超马丁戈形式化SCARCE，展示了其在MNIST误分类等任务上的有效性，显著降低了与传统SS相比的平均绝对误差。此外，SCARCE还被应用于分析PAIR风格的LLM越狱，显示出良好的性能和低相对误差率，表明其在稀有事件表征中的广泛应用潜力。
Sparse Autoencoders are Capable LLM Jailbreak Mitigators
Authors: Yannick Assogba, Jacopo Cortellazzi, Javier Abad, Pau Rodriguez, Xavier Suau, Arno Blaas
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-12T21:17:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-27T01:07:47+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the Mechanistic Interpretability Workshop, ICML 2026. 31 pages, 20 figures, 7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Jailbreak attacks remain a persistent threat to large language model safety. We propose Context-Conditioned Delta Steering (CC-Delta), an SAE-based defense that identifies jailbreak-relevant sparse features by comparing token-level representations of the same harmful request with and without jailbreak context. Using paired harmful/jailbreak prompts, CC-Delta selects features via statistical testing and applies inference-time mean-shift steering in SAE latent space. Across four aligned instruction-tuned models and twelve jailbreak attacks, CC-Delta achieves comparable or better safety-utility tradeoffs than baseline defenses operating in dense latent space. In particular, our method clearly outperforms dense mean-shift steering on all four models, and particularly against out-of-distribution attacks, showing that steering in sparse SAE feature space offers advantages over steering in dense activation space for jailbreak mitigation. Our results suggest off-the-shelf SAEs trained for interpretability can be repurposed as practical jailbreak defenses without task-specific training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：稀疏自编码器能够缓解大型语言模型的越狱攻击
越狱攻击仍然是大型语言模型安全的持续威胁。我们提出了上下文条件的增量引导（CC-Delta），这是一种基于稀疏自编码器的防御方法，通过比较同一有害请求在有无越狱上下文下的令牌级表示，识别与越狱相关的稀疏特征。使用成对的有害/越狱提示，CC-Delta通过统计测试选择特征，并在稀疏自编码器潜在空间中应用推理时均值偏移引导。在四个对齐的指令调优模型和十二个越狱攻击中，CC-Delta实现了与在稠密潜在空间中运行的基线防御相当或更好的安全效用权衡。特别是，我们的方法在所有四个模型上明显优于稠密均值偏移引导，尤其是在对抗分布外攻击时，表明在稀疏自编码器特征空间中的引导相较于在稠密激活空间中的引导在越狱缓解方面具有优势。我们的结果表明，经过可解释性训练的现成稀疏自编码器可以被重新用于作为实用的越狱防御，而无需特定任务的训练。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the ongoing challenge of jailbreak attacks on large language models, which pose significant safety risks. The proposed Context-Conditioned Delta Steering (CC-Delta) method utilizes Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to identify and mitigate jailbreak threats by analyzing token-level representations of harmful requests in both jailbreak and non-jailbreak contexts. Just Ask: Curious Code Agents Reveal System Prompts in Frontier LLMs
Authors: Xiang Zheng, Yutao Wu, Hanxun Huang, Yige Li, Xingjun Ma, Bo Li, Yu-Gang Jiang, Cong Wang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-01-29T03:53:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T10:38:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous code agents built on large language models are reshaping software and AI development through tool use, long-horizon reasoning, and self-directed interaction. However, this autonomy introduces a previously unrecognized security risk: agentic interaction fundamentally expands the LLM attack surface, enabling systematic probing and recovery of hidden system prompts that guide model behavior. We identify system prompt extraction as an emergent vulnerability intrinsic to code agents and present \textbf{\textsc{JustAsk}}, a self-evolving framework that autonomously discovers effective extraction strategies through interaction alone. Unlike prior prompt-engineering or dataset-based attacks, \textsc{JustAsk} requires no handcrafted prompts, labeled supervision, or privileged access beyond standard user interaction. It formulates extraction as an online exploration problem, using Upper Confidence Bound-based strategy selection and a hierarchical skill space spanning atomic probes and high-level orchestration. These skills exploit imperfect system-instruction generalization and inherent tensions between helpfulness and safety. Evaluated on \textbf{41} black-box commercial models across multiple providers, \textsc{JustAsk} consistently achieves full or near-complete system prompt recovery, revealing recurring design- and architecture-level vulnerabilities. Our results expose system prompts as a critical yet largely unprotected attack surface in modern agent systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：只需询问：好奇的代码代理揭示前沿大型语言模型中的系统提示
基于大型语言模型的自主代码代理通过工具使用、长远推理和自我导向交互正在重塑软件和人工智能开发。然而，这种自主性引入了一个之前未被认识的安全风险：代理交互从根本上扩展了大型语言模型的攻击面，使得系统提示的系统性探测和恢复成为可能，这些提示指导模型行为。我们将系统提示提取识别为代码代理内在的新兴漏洞，并提出\textbf{\textsc{JustAsk}}，一个通过交互自主发现有效提取策略的自我演化框架。与之前的提示工程或基于数据集的攻击不同，\textsc{JustAsk}不需要手工提示、标记监督或超出标准用户交互的特权访问。它将提取形式化为在线探索问题，使用基于上置信界的策略选择和跨越原子探测和高层编排的层次技能空间。这些技能利用了系统指令泛化的不完美性以及有用性与安全性之间的固有紧张关系。在对\textbf{41}个来自多个提供商的黑箱商业模型进行评估时，\textsc{JustAsk}始终实现了完整或近乎完整的系统提示恢复，揭示了反复出现的设计和架构级漏洞。我们的结果揭示了系统提示作为现代代理系统中一个关键但在很大程度上未受保护的攻击面。
